.cols_ds <- names(py_corrected_datasets[[1]])
.strata_excluded <- c("tr_outcome_adm_discharge_rule_violation_undet")
.is_strata_var <- function(v) startsWith(v, "plan_type") | (v %in% .strata_excluded)

# These seven shortlisted names are families, not literal columns. The expected
# sizes were verified against pred21_ndp_2026_03_26.Rdata. A changed coding now
# stops the notebook instead of silently estimating a different specification.
.expected_family_sizes <- c(
  eva_consumo            = 2L,
  eva_sm                  = 2L,
  ed_attainment_corr      = 2L,
  prim_sub_freq_rec       = 2L,
  eva_ocupacion           = 2L,
  eva_fisica              = 2L,
  tenure_status_household = 4L
)

.resolve_feature_terms <- function(feature, cols = .cols_ds) {
  feature <- as.character(feature)

  if (length(feature) != 1L || is.na(feature) || !nzchar(feature)) {
    stop("Invalid empty feature name in the interaction shortlist.", call. = FALSE)
  }
  if (.is_strata_var(feature)) {
    stop(sprintf("Stratification variable '%s' reached the interaction builder.", feature),
         call. = FALSE)
  }

  # A literal column always retains its original coding. Thus a selected dummy,
  # such as tr_outcome_referral, is not expanded to the whole tr_outcome family.
  if (feature %in% cols) return(feature)

  if (!(feature %in% names(.expected_family_sizes))) {
    stop(sprintf(
      "Feature '%s' is not a data column and has no approved family expansion.",
      feature
    ), call. = FALSE)
  }

  hits <- cols[startsWith(cols, paste0(feature, "_"))]
  hits <- hits[!grepl("(_score|_any|_strata)$", hits)]
  hits <- sort(unique(hits))

  expected_n <- unname(.expected_family_sizes[[feature]])
  if (length(hits) != expected_n) {
    stop(sprintf(
      "Family '%s' resolved to %d column(s), but %d were expected. Found: %s",
      feature, length(hits), expected_n,
      if (length(hits)) paste(hits, collapse = ", ") else "none"
    ), call. = FALSE)
  }
  hits
}

.assert_columns_in_all_imputations <- function(cols) {
  missing_log <- unlist(lapply(seq_along(py_corrected_datasets), function(m) {
    miss <- setdiff(cols, names(py_corrected_datasets[[m]]))
    if (length(miss)) sprintf("imputation %d: %s", m, paste(miss, collapse = ", "))
  }))
  if (length(missing_log)) {
    stop(paste("Resolved columns are absent from some imputations:",
               paste(missing_log, collapse = " | ")), call. = FALSE)
  }
  invisible(TRUE)
}

.normalise_interaction <- function(term) {
  parts <- strsplit(term, ":", fixed = TRUE)[[1]]
  paste(sort(parts), collapse = ":")
}

.build_interactions <- function(base_formula, rows, outcome_label) {
  if (is.null(rows) || !nrow(rows)) {
    return(list(
      formula = base_formula,
      audit = data.frame(),
      expansion_map = data.frame()
    ))
  }

  pair_key <- paste(rows$main_feature, rows$interactor, sep = " || ")
  if (anyDuplicated(pair_key)) {
    stop(sprintf("%s shortlist contains duplicated candidate pairs.", outcome_label),
         call. = FALSE)
  }

  all_main_terms <- character(0)
  all_interactions <- character(0)
  audit_rows <- vector("list", nrow(rows))
  map_rows <- list()

  for (i in seq_len(nrow(rows))) {
    a <- as.character(rows$main_feature[[i]])
    b <- as.character(rows$interactor[[i]])
    a_terms <- .resolve_feature_terms(a)
    b_terms <- .resolve_feature_terms(b)

    .assert_columns_in_all_imputations(c(a_terms, b_terms))

    interaction_terms <- as.vector(outer(
      a_terms, b_terms,
      FUN = function(x, y) paste(x, y, sep = ":")
    ))

    if (!length(interaction_terms)) {
      stop(sprintf("Candidate '%s x %s' produced no interaction terms.", a, b),
           call. = FALSE)
    }

    all_main_terms <- c(all_main_terms, a_terms, b_terms)
    all_interactions <- c(all_interactions, interaction_terms)

    audit_rows[[i]] <- data.frame(
      outcome = outcome_label,
      main_feature = a,
      interactor = b,
      main_columns = paste(a_terms, collapse = " + "),
      interactor_columns = paste(b_terms, collapse = " + "),
      n_interaction_terms = length(interaction_terms),
      status = "ADDED",
      stringsAsFactors = FALSE
    )

    map_rows[[length(map_rows) + 1L]] <- data.frame(
      outcome = outcome_label,
      main_feature = a,
      interactor = b,
      cox_interaction_term = interaction_terms,
      stringsAsFactors = FALSE
    )
  }

  all_main_terms <- unique(all_main_terms)
  all_interactions <- unique(all_interactions)
  add_terms <- c(all_main_terms, all_interactions)

  if (any(make.names(all_main_terms) != all_main_terms)) {
    stop("Non-syntactic column names require explicit quoting; review before fitting.",
         call. = FALSE)
  }

  updated <- stats::update(
    base_formula,
    stats::as.formula(
      paste(". ~ . +", paste(add_terms, collapse = " + ")),
      env = environment(base_formula)
    )
  )
  updated <- stats::as.formula(
    gsub("strat\\(", "strata(", paste(deparse(updated, width.cutoff = 500L), collapse = " ")),
    env = environment(base_formula)
  )

  # Failable verification: every candidate product and both components must be
  # present in the resulting formula. Interaction order is normalised because R
  # may print a:b or b:a without changing the fitted model.
  labels <- attr(stats::terms(updated), "term.labels")
  actual_interactions <- grep(":", labels, fixed = TRUE, value = TRUE)
  missing_interactions <- setdiff(
    vapply(all_interactions, .normalise_interaction, character(1)),
    vapply(actual_interactions, .normalise_interaction, character(1))
  )
  missing_main <- setdiff(all_main_terms, labels)

  if (length(missing_interactions) || length(missing_main)) {
    stop(sprintf(
      "%s formula failed the expansion audit. Missing main effects: %s. Missing interactions: %s.",
      outcome_label,
      if (length(missing_main)) paste(missing_main, collapse = ", ") else "none",
      if (length(missing_interactions)) paste(missing_interactions, collapse = ", ") else "none"
    ), call. = FALSE)
  }

  list(
    formula = updated,
    audit = do.call(rbind, audit_rows),
    expansion_map = do.call(rbind, map_rows)
  )
}

.short_readmit <- dplyr::filter(top_interactions, outcome == "Readmission")
.short_death <- dplyr::filter(top_interactions, outcome == "Death")

# These counts freeze the current DS72 shortlist. If the upstream selection is
# intentionally changed, review the new list before changing these expectations.
if (nrow(.short_readmit) != 14L || nrow(.short_death) != 18L) {
  stop(sprintf(
    "Unexpected shortlist size: readmission=%d (expected 14), death=%d (expected 18).",
    nrow(.short_readmit), nrow(.short_death)
  ), call. = FALSE)
}

.built_readmit <- .build_interactions(
  formula_shap_ds_readmit_clean_updated, .short_readmit, "Readmission"
)
.built_death <- .build_interactions(
  formula_shap_ds_death_updated, .short_death, "Death"
)

formula_shap_ds_readmit_interact <- .built_readmit$formula
formula_shap_ds_death_interact <- .built_death$formula

interaction_expansion_audit <- rbind(.built_readmit$audit, .built_death$audit)
interaction_expansion_map <- rbind(.built_readmit$expansion_map,
                                   .built_death$expansion_map)

# With the current shortlist, the 14 readmission pairs expand to 18 Cox product
# terms and the 18 mortality pairs expand to 26. This check catches a recurrence
# of the ten silent omissions as well as an unintended change in family coding.
.expanded_counts <- aggregate(
  n_interaction_terms ~ outcome,
  data = interaction_expansion_audit,
  FUN = sum
)
.n_readmit <- .expanded_counts$n_interaction_terms[
  .expanded_counts$outcome == "Readmission"
]
.n_death <- .expanded_counts$n_interaction_terms[
  .expanded_counts$outcome == "Death"
]
if (!identical(as.integer(.n_readmit), 18L) ||
    !identical(as.integer(.n_death), 26L)) {
  stop(sprintf(
    "Unexpected expansion: readmission=%s terms (expected 18), death=%s terms (expected 26).",
    paste(.n_readmit, collapse = ","), paste(.n_death, collapse = ",")
  ), call. = FALSE)
}

cat("\nCandidate-pair expansion audit:\n")
print(interaction_expansion_audit, row.names = FALSE)
cat(sprintf(
  "\nVerified: %d/%d readmission pairs -> %d Cox interaction terms; %d/%d death pairs -> %d terms.\n",
  nrow(.built_readmit$audit), nrow(.short_readmit), .n_readmit,
  nrow(.built_death$audit), nrow(.short_death), .n_death
))
cat("No shortlisted pair was silently skipped.\n\n")

print(formula_shap_ds_readmit_interact)
print(formula_shap_ds_death_interact)

.elapsed <- proc.time() - .t0
cat(sprintf(
  "\n[chunk: %s] %.1f sec (%.1f min)\n",
  "shap-criteria-int2", .elapsed[["elapsed"]], .elapsed[["elapsed"]] / 60
))

.t0 <- proc.time()

# Collinearity guardrail for the interaction models. NOTE: product/interaction terms inflate
# GVIF by construction, so focus on MAIN-effect families. Rule: if a MAIN-effect category has
# GVIF >= 10 (alongside unstable SEs / non-convergence), drop that SPECIFIC category (not the
# whole family) and re-check -- do not discard the family automatically.
for (.nm in c("readmit", "death")) {
  .f <- if (.nm == "readmit") formula_shap_ds_readmit_interact else formula_shap_ds_death_interact
  .fit <- try(survival::coxph(.f, data = py_corrected_datasets[[1]]), silent = TRUE)
  if (inherits(.fit, "try-error")) { cat(sprintf("\n[%s interactions] model not evaluable\n", .nm)); next }
  .v <- try(rms::vif(.fit), silent = TRUE)
  if (inherits(.v, "try-error")) { cat(sprintf("\n[%s interactions] vif() failed\n", .nm)); next }
  hi <- .v[.v >= 10]
  cat(sprintf("\n[%s interactions] terms with GVIF >= 10 (%d):\n", .nm, length(hi)))
  if (length(hi)) print(round(sort(hi, decreasing = TRUE), 2)) else cat("  none\n")
}

if (!exists(".t0") || !inherits(.t0, "proc_time")) .t0 <- proc.time()
.elapsed <- proc.time() - .t0
cat(sprintf("\n[chunk: %s] %.1f sec (%.1f min)\n", "shap-criteria-int-gvif", .elapsed["elapsed"], .elapsed["elapsed"]/60))

.t0 <- proc.time()

# Collinearity guardrail for the interaction models. NOTE: product/interaction terms inflate
# GVIF by construction, so focus on MAIN-effect families. Rule: if a MAIN-effect category has
# GVIF >= 10 (alongside unstable SEs / non-convergence), drop that SPECIFIC category (not the
# whole family) and re-check -- do not discard the family automatically.
for (.nm in c("readmit", "death")) {
  .f <- if (.nm == "readmit") formula_shap_ds_readmit_interact else formula_shap_ds_death_interact
  .fit <- try(survival::coxph(.f, data = py_corrected_datasets[[1]]), silent = TRUE)
  if (inherits(.fit, "try-error")) { cat(sprintf("\n[%s interactions] model not evaluable\n", .nm)); next }
  .v <- try(rms::vif(.fit), silent = TRUE)
  if (inherits(.v, "try-error")) { cat(sprintf("\n[%s interactions] vif() failed\n", .nm)); next }
  hi <- .v[.v >= 10]
  cat(sprintf("\n[%s interactions] terms with GVIF >= 10 (%d):\n", .nm, length(hi)))
  if (length(hi)) print(round(sort(hi, decreasing = TRUE), 2)) else cat("  none\n")
}

if (!exists(".t0") || !inherits(.t0, "proc_time")) .t0 <- proc.time()
.elapsed <- proc.time() - .t0
cat(sprintf("\n[chunk: %s] %.1f sec (%.1f min)\n", "shap-criteria-int-gvif", .elapsed["elapsed"], .elapsed["elapsed"]/60))

.t0 <- proc.time()
## =============================================================================
## Collinearity guardrail for the INTERACTION models. Rewritten after an audit that
## exposed two defects in the previous version.
##
## DEFECT 1, SILENT SKIP. coxph names the coefficient of a logical or factor component
##   with a level suffix: dx_f_any_severe_mental -> dx_f_any_severe_mentalTRUE. The old
##   matcher compared coefficient-name parts to term labels by exact equality, so those
##   terms resolved to a NA label and were dropped by `v_int[!is.na(labs)]`. They never
##   entered the screen and never appeared in the log. In the readmission model this
##   silently skipped adm_age_rec3:dx_f_any_severe_mental, whose GVIF was 11.38, i.e.
##   ABOVE the threshold the screen was supposed to enforce.
##   FIX: map each coefficient-name part to the data column that is its LONGEST PREFIX.
##   That is unambiguous, immune to level suffixes, and safe when one variable name is a
##   prefix of another, because the longest match wins.
##
## DEFECT 2, ASYMMETRIC ARTIFACT TEST. The centering test was applied only to interactions
##   with a CONTINUOUS component, because is.numeric() is FALSE for logicals. Dummy by dummy
##   terms were routed to a different escape hatch, the cross-cell event count, which answers
##   a DIFFERENT question: sparse-data bias, not collinearity. But a product term is
##   mechanically correlated with its own main effect whenever the components are not
##   centered, and that holds for binary components exactly as it does for continuous ones.
##   The audit confirmed it: the four coefficients above 10 in the readmission model came in
##   two pairs, each a main effect with the product that contains it, and all four collapsed
##   under centering (11.42 -> 1.12, 11.38 -> 1.04, 10.98 -> 1.67, 10.73 -> 1.39).
##   FIX: centering is now THE collinearity criterion for EVERY interaction. Under strong
##   hierarchy, which is checked below, (a - abar)(b - bbar) is a linear combination of ab,
##   a, b and 1, so the centered parameterization spans the SAME column space: the fit is
##   unchanged, verified here by the log-likelihood gap, and only the parameterization moves.
##   A GVIF that collapses was an artifact and the term stays. A GVIF that persists is
##   substantive collinearity and the term is removed.
##
## The cross-cell support check is KEPT, but as an INDEPENDENT guardrail applied to every
## dummy by dummy interaction (about 10 events per parameter, Peduzzi 1996; sparse-data bias,
## Greenland 2016), not as an escape hatch coupled to the GVIF threshold. Set
## SPARSE_CHECK_ALL <- FALSE to restore the legacy coupling.
## =============================================================================
suppressPackageStartupMessages({ library(survival); library(rms) })
GVIF_THR         <- 10
MIN_CELL_EVENTS  <- 10L
SPARSE_CHECK_ALL <- TRUE
## ---- name mapping: coefficient name -> data columns, robust to level suffixes ----------
.longest_prefix_var <- function(part, varnames) {
  hits <- varnames[startsWith(part, varnames)]
  if (!length(hits)) return(NA_character_)
  hits[which.max(nchar(hits))]
}
.coef_vars <- function(cn, varnames) {
  parts <- strsplit(cn, ":", fixed = TRUE)[[1]]
  vv <- vapply(parts, .longest_prefix_var, character(1), varnames = varnames)
  if (anyNA(vv)) NA_character_ else sort(unname(vv))
}
.gvif_of <- function(v, parts, varnames) {
  if (!length(v)) return(NA_real_)
  key <- sort(parts)
  m <- vapply(names(v), function(cn) identical(.coef_vars(cn, varnames), key), logical(1))
  if (any(m)) max(v[m]) else NA_real_
}
## ---- centering: coerce logical / 2-level factor to 0-1, then subtract the mean ---------
.center_components <- function(d, vars) {
  bad <- character(0)
  for (p in vars) {
    x <- d[[p]]
    if (is.logical(x)) x <- as.numeric(x)
    if (is.factor(x) && nlevels(x) == 2L) x <- as.numeric(x) - 1
    if (is.numeric(x)) d[[p]] <- x - mean(x, na.rm = TRUE) else bad <- c(bad, p)
  }
  list(data = d, uncenterable = bad)
}
.rebuild <- function(f, keep) as.formula(
  paste(paste(deparse(f[[2]]), collapse = ""), "~", paste(keep, collapse = " + ")), env = environment(f))
.cell_support <- function(d, parts, event_col) {
  ok <- rep(TRUE, nrow(d))
  for (p in parts) ok <- ok & (as.numeric(d[[p]]) == 1)
  e <- as.numeric(d[[event_col]]); e[is.na(e)] <- 0
  c(events = sum(e[ok] == 1, na.rm = TRUE), obs = sum(ok, na.rm = TRUE))
}
.is_binary <- function(x) length(unique(x[!is.na(x)])) <= 2L
prune_interactions_collinearity <- function(f, data, event_col, thr = GVIF_THR,
                                            min_cell_events = MIN_CELL_EVENTS,
                                            sparse_check_all = SPARSE_CHECK_ALL,
                                            max_iter = 200L, label = "") {
  logs <- list()
  .add <- function(row) logs[[length(logs) + 1L]] <<- row
  vn0 <- intersect(all.vars(f), names(data))
  tl0 <- attr(terms(f), "term.labels")
  ints0 <- grep(":", tl0, fixed = TRUE, value = TRUE)
  cat(sprintf("\n===== [%s] interaction screen: %d interaction term(s) =====\n", label, length(ints0)))
  ## ---- strong hierarchy: the centering test is only valid if both main effects are in ----
  for (lab in ints0) {
    miss <- setdiff(strsplit(lab, ":", fixed = TRUE)[[1]], tl0)
    if (length(miss))
      cat(sprintf("[%s] WARNING: '%s' breaks strong hierarchy (main effect absent: %s). The centering\n           test assumes both main effects are present; its GVIF is not interpretable as an artifact test.\n",
                  label, lab, paste(miss, collapse = ", ")))
  }
  ## ---- guardrail 1: cross-cell support, an INDEPENDENT sparse-data check -----------------
  f_cur <- f
  if (isTRUE(sparse_check_all)) {
    tl <- attr(terms(f_cur), "term.labels")
    for (lab in grep(":", tl, fixed = TRUE, value = TRUE)) {
      parts <- strsplit(lab, ":", fixed = TRUE)[[1]]
      if (!all(parts %in% names(data))) next
      if (!all(vapply(parts, function(p) .is_binary(data[[p]]), logical(1)))) next
      sup <- .cell_support(data, parts, event_col)
      if (sup[["events"]] < min_cell_events) {
        f_cur <- .rebuild(f_cur, setdiff(attr(terms(f_cur), "term.labels"), lab))
        .add(data.frame(term = lab, check = "cross-cell support", gvif_raw = NA_real_, gvif_centered = NA_real_,
                        cell_events = sup[["events"]], cell_obs = sup[["obs"]],
                        action = sprintf("REMOVE (sparse 1x1 cell: %d events / %d obs < %d)",
                                         sup[["events"]], sup[["obs"]], min_cell_events), stringsAsFactors = FALSE))
      } else {
        .add(data.frame(term = lab, check = "cross-cell support", gvif_raw = NA_real_, gvif_centered = NA_real_,
                        cell_events = sup[["events"]], cell_obs = sup[["obs"]],
                        action = sprintf("KEEP (cell OK: %d events / %d obs)", sup[["events"]], sup[["obs"]]),
                        stringsAsFactors = FALSE))
      }
    }
  }
  ## ---- guardrail 2: collinearity, judged on the CENTERED parameterization ----------------
  ## Iterative, because removing one term changes the GVIF of the others.
  for (it in seq_len(max_iter)) {
    tl <- attr(terms(f_cur), "term.labels")
    ints <- grep(":", tl, fixed = TRUE, value = TRUE)
    if (!length(ints)) break
    fit <- try(coxph(f_cur, data = data), silent = TRUE)
    if (inherits(fit, "try-error")) { cat(sprintf("[%s] model not evaluable; stopping.\n", label)); break }
    parts_all <- unique(unlist(strsplit(ints, ":", fixed = TRUE)))
    parts_all <- intersect(parts_all, names(data))
    cc <- .center_components(data, parts_all)
    if (length(cc$uncenterable))
      cat(sprintf("[%s] NOTE: cannot center %s (multi-level factor). Its interactions are screened on the raw GVIF only.\n",
                  label, paste(cc$uncenterable, collapse = ", ")))
    fit_c <- try(coxph(f_cur, data = cc$data), silent = TRUE)
    if (inherits(fit_c, "try-error")) { cat(sprintf("[%s] centered refit failed; stopping.\n", label)); break }
    ll_gap <- abs(fit$loglik[2] - fit_c$loglik[2])
    if (ll_gap > 1e-6)
      cat(sprintf("[%s] WARNING: log-likelihood gap = %.3g between raw and centered fits. Centering should be a pure\n           reparameterization; a nonzero gap means the artifact argument does NOT hold here.\n", label, ll_gap))
    v_raw <- try(rms::vif(fit),   silent = TRUE); if (inherits(v_raw, "try-error")) break
    v_cen <- try(rms::vif(fit_c), silent = TRUE); if (inherits(v_cen, "try-error")) break
    vn <- intersect(all.vars(f_cur), names(data))
    g_raw <- vapply(ints, function(l) .gvif_of(v_raw, strsplit(l, ":", fixed = TRUE)[[1]], vn), numeric(1))
    g_cen <- vapply(ints, function(l) .gvif_of(v_cen, strsplit(l, ":", fixed = TRUE)[[1]], vn), numeric(1))
    if (anyNA(g_raw))
      cat(sprintf("[%s] WARNING: no GVIF could be matched for: %s. These terms were NOT screened.\n",
                  label, paste(ints[is.na(g_raw)], collapse = ", ")))
    offend <- ints[is.finite(g_cen) & g_cen >= thr]
    if (!length(offend)) {
      for (l in ints) .add(data.frame(term = l, check = "collinearity (centered)", gvif_raw = round(g_raw[[l]], 2),
                                      gvif_centered = round(g_cen[[l]], 2), cell_events = NA_integer_, cell_obs = NA_integer_,
                                      action = if (is.na(g_cen[[l]])) "KEEP (GVIF not evaluable)"
                                               else if (g_raw[[l]] >= thr) "KEEP (raw GVIF high but collapses on centering: product-term artifact)"
                                               else "KEEP (GVIF below threshold)", stringsAsFactors = FALSE))
      break
    }
    worst <- offend[which.max(g_cen[offend])]
    .add(data.frame(term = worst, check = "collinearity (centered)", gvif_raw = round(g_raw[[worst]], 2),
                    gvif_centered = round(g_cen[[worst]], 2), cell_events = NA_integer_, cell_obs = NA_integer_,
                    action = "REMOVE (collinearity persists after centering: substantive, not an artifact)",
                    stringsAsFactors = FALSE))
    f_cur <- .rebuild(f_cur, setdiff(tl, worst))
  }
  log_df <- if (length(logs)) do.call(rbind, logs) else
    data.frame(term = character(0), check = character(0), gvif_raw = numeric(0), gvif_centered = numeric(0),
               cell_events = integer(0), cell_obs = integer(0), action = character(0))
  list(formula = f_cur, log = log_df)
}
## ---- run on both outcomes ---------------------------------------------------------------
.specs <- list(readmit = list(f = formula_shap_ds_readmit_interact, ev = "readmit_event"),
               death   = list(f = formula_shap_ds_death_interact,   ev = "death_event"))
res_int_vif <- list()
for (nm in names(.specs)) {
  out <- prune_interactions_collinearity(.specs[[nm]]$f, py_corrected_datasets[[1]],
                                         event_col = .specs[[nm]]$ev, label = nm)
  res_int_vif[[nm]] <- out
  cat(sprintf("\n----- [%s] screening log (GVIF >= %g on the CENTERED parameterization; cross-cell < %d events) -----\n",
              nm, GVIF_THR, MIN_CELL_EVENTS))
  if (nrow(out$log)) print(out$log, row.names = FALSE) else cat("No interaction was screened.\n")
  ints_left <- grep(":", attr(terms(out$formula), "term.labels"), fixed = TRUE, value = TRUE)
  cat(sprintf("[%s] interactions remaining (%d):\n", nm, length(ints_left)))
  if (length(ints_left)) cat(paste0("  ", ints_left), sep = "\n") else cat("  (none)\n")
  ## final audit of the surviving model: the number that goes into the manuscript
  .fit_f <- coxph(out$formula, data = py_corrected_datasets[[1]])
  .p <- intersect(unique(unlist(strsplit(ints_left, ":", fixed = TRUE))), names(py_corrected_datasets[[1]]))
  .fit_fc <- coxph(out$formula, data = .center_components(py_corrected_datasets[[1]], .p)$data)
  cat(sprintf("[%s] FINAL max GVIF over all coefficients: raw = %.2f | centered = %.2f | threshold = %g | loglik gap = %.3g\n",
              nm, max(rms::vif(.fit_f), na.rm = TRUE), max(rms::vif(.fit_fc), na.rm = TRUE), GVIF_THR,
              abs(.fit_f$loglik[2] - .fit_fc$loglik[2])))
}
formula_shap_ds_readmit_interact_vif <- res_int_vif[["readmit"]]$formula
formula_shap_ds_death_interact_vif   <- res_int_vif[["death"]]$formula
.elapsed <- proc.time() - .t0
cat(sprintf("\n[chunk: %s] %.1f sec (%.1f min)\n", "shap-criteria-int-gvif-prune", .elapsed["elapsed"], .elapsed["elapsed"]/60))

<environment: 0x0000012aa279c908>

For the DeepSurv sensitivity models, no interactions were removed. GVIF values above 10 did not indicate substantive collinearity.

In the readmission model, one dummy-by-dummy interaction had GVIF = 10.73, but its joint cell was well supported, with 206 events among 1,066 observations. It was therefore retained.

In the mortality model, two age interactions had elevated GVIFs, 14.94 and 12.63. After centering age, these dropped to 1.35 and 1.05, showing that the inflation was only a product-term artifact. Both were retained.

Overall, the GVIF diagnostics supported keeping all interactions.

Code 
.t0 <- proc.time()

# Fit Cox model with the interaction formula
cox_shap_ds_death_int <- coxph(
  formula = formula_shap_ds_death_interact_vif,
  data = py_corrected_datasets[[1]],   # replace with your actual death dataset
  ties = "efron",
  x = TRUE
)

# Proportional hazards test
zph_shap_ds_death_int <- cox.zph(
  cox_shap_ds_death_int,
  transform = "km",
  terms = TRUE,
  global = TRUE
)
if (!exists(".t0") || !inherits(.t0, "proc_time")) .t0 <- proc.time()

  dplyr::mutate(

    Metric = ifelse(Metric == "Uno's C-Index", "Discrimination (C-index)", "Calibration (IBS)"),
    Risk = factor(Risk, levels = c("Readmission", "Death")),
    x_offset = dplyr::case_when(

    )
  )

.t0 <- proc.time()

# Conservative keep/drop for interactions: ADOPT only with >= 0.01 C or IBS gain and no material
# worsening; otherwise keep the simpler (no-interaction) model. Interactions are NOT kept just
# because SHAP/ds72 suggested them or AICc improved. Calibration is verified in the next cell.

# Compare the anchor vs candidate for each concordance score
for (score in c("lp", "risk")) {
  message("\n=== Decision for concordance_score = '", score, "' ===")
  .decide_dualcox(
    results_ds_shap_py_upd$results[[score]],
    results_ds_shap_py_upd_int$results[[score]],
    anchor_label = paste("rule (extra strata,", score, ")"),
    cand_label   = paste("rule + NPH strata + interactions,", score, ")")
  )
}

# Use the portable worker cap and allow parallelism only at the cross-validation level.                                         
if (!exists("sistrat_workers", inherits = FALSE) || !           
exists("sistrat_future_max_globals", inherits = FALSE))         
stop("Run select-portable-r-library first.", call. = FALSE)     
.with_sistrat_workers <- function(expr) {                       
.previous_plan <- future::plan()                              
on.exit(future::plan(.previous_plan), add = TRUE)             
future::plan(future::multisession, workers = sistrat_workers) 
options(future.globals.maxSize = sistrat_future_max_globals)  
force(expr)                                                   
}

# 1. Identify D_60m_* and R_60m_* sheets
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
all_sheets    <- readxl::excel_sheets(fx_form_path)
target_sheets <- all_sheets[stringr::str_detect(all_sheets, "^[DR]_60m_")]
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Map truncated sheet names back to full Predictor names
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
summary_df <- readxl::read_excel(fx_form_path, sheet = "Effects_Summary_Horizon")

sheet_to_predictor <- purrr::map_chr(target_sheets, function(sheet) {
  suffix     <- stringr::str_remove(sheet, "^[DR]_60m_")
  candidates <- summary_df$Predictor[startsWith(summary_df$Predictor, suffix)]
  if (length(candidates) == 1) candidates else suffix
})
names(sheet_to_predictor) <- target_sheets
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Read all target sheets and stack into one long data frame
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
spc_tbl_ <- purrr::map_dfr(target_sheets, function(sheet) {
  outcome <- if (stringr::str_starts(sheet, "D_60m_")) "Death" else "Readmission"
  
  readxl::read_excel(fx_form_path, sheet = sheet) |>
    dplyr::mutate(
      Outcome   = outcome,
      Predictor = sheet_to_predictor[[sheet]],
      .before   = 1
    )
})
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Normalization / binning / summary (exact logic you provided)
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
shap_norm <- spc_tbl_ |>
  dplyr::filter(
    is.finite(Feature_Value),
    is.finite(SHAP_Impact)
  ) |>
  dplyr::group_by(Predictor) |>
  dplyr::mutate(
    n_unique = dplyr::n_distinct(Feature_Value),
    Feature_Value_norm = dplyr::case_when(
      n_unique <= 1 ~ 0.5,
      n_unique <= 10 ~ (dplyr::dense_rank(Feature_Value) - 1) / (n_unique - 1),
      TRUE ~ dplyr::percent_rank(Feature_Value)
    )
  ) |>
  dplyr::ungroup()

shap_curve_norm <- shap_norm |>
  dplyr::group_by(Outcome, Predictor) |>
  dplyr::mutate(value_bin = dplyr::ntile(Feature_Value_norm, 50)) |>
  dplyr::group_by(Outcome, Predictor, value_bin) |>
  dplyr::summarise(
    x_norm       = median(Feature_Value_norm, na.rm = TRUE),
    x_raw_median = median(Feature_Value, na.rm = TRUE),
    shap_median  = median(SHAP_Impact, na.rm = TRUE),
    shap_q25     = quantile(SHAP_Impact, 0.25, na.rm = TRUE),
    shap_q75     = quantile(SHAP_Impact, 0.75, na.rm = TRUE),
    .groups      = "drop"
  )

if (!exists(".t0") || !inherits(.t0, "proc_time")) .t0 <- proc.time()
.elapsed <- proc.time() - .t0
cat(sprintf("\n[chunk: %s] %.1f sec (%.1f min)\n", "shap-criteria-func-form-get-shap-values", .elapsed["elapsed"], .elapsed["elapsed"]/60))
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Plot (exact ggplot code you provided)
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
ggplot2::ggplot(dplyr::filter(shap_curve_norm, !grepl("eva_*",Predictor)), 
ggplot2::aes(x = x_norm, y = shap_median)) +
  ggplot2::geom_ribbon(
    ggplot2::aes(ymin = shap_q25, ymax = shap_q75),
    alpha = 0.2, fill = "grey70"
  ) +
  ggplot2::geom_line(linewidth = 0.8, colour = "#1f77b4") +
  ggplot2::geom_point(size = 0.8, colour = "#1f77b4") +
  ggplot2::geom_hline(yintercept = 0, linetype = "dashed", colour = "grey40") +
  ggplot2::facet_grid(Predictor ~ Outcome, scales = "free_y") +
  ggplot2::scale_x_continuous(
    labels = scales::percent_format(),
    limits = c(0, 1)
  ) +
  ggplot2::labs(
    x = "Normalized feature value",
    y = "Median SHAP influence",
    title = "Normalized functional form of predictors by model outcome"
  ) +
  ggplot2::theme_minimal(base_size = 11) +
  ggplot2::theme(
    strip.text.y   = ggplot2::element_text(angle = 0),
    panel.grid.minor = ggplot2::element_blank()
  )

## 1. Spanish labels and panel order --------------------------------------------
.lab_pred <- c(
  adm_age_rec3                  = "Edad al ingreso",
  dit_m                         = "Tiempo de estadía (meses)",
  ed_attainment_corr            = "Escolaridad",
  eva_consumo                   = "Egreso: patrón de consumo",
  eva_fam                       = "Egreso: situación familiar",
  eva_fisica                    = "Egreso: salud física",
  eva_ocupacion                 = "Egreso: situación ocupacional",
  eva_relinterp                 = "Egreso: relaciones interpersonales",
  eva_sm                        = "Egreso: salud mental",
  eva_transgnorma               = "Egreso: transgresión a la norma",
  evaluacindelprocesoteraputico = "Egreso: proceso terapéutico",
  porc_pobr                     = "Índice de pobreza comunal",
  prim_sub_freq_rec             = "Frecuencia de consumo al ingreso",
  tenure_status_household       = "Situación de vivienda",
  urbanicity_cat                = "Urbanización de la comuna"
)
## Restore identifiers truncated to 15 characters in an earlier processing step
.predictor_alias <- c(
  ed_attainment_c = "ed_attainment_corr",
  evaluacindelpro = "evaluacindelprocesoteraputico",
  prim_sub_freq_r = "prim_sub_freq_rec",
  tenure_status_h = "tenure_status_household"
)
## Canonicalise predictor and outcome identifiers without dropping rows
.plot_source <- shap_curve_norm |>
  dplyr::mutate(
    Predictor_original = as.character(Predictor),
    Predictor_key = dplyr::coalesce(
      unname(.predictor_alias[Predictor_original]),
      Predictor_original
    ),
    Outcome_key = as.character(Outcome)
  )
## Fail if any observed predictor has no Spanish label
.unlabelled <- setdiff(
  unique(.plot_source$Predictor_key),
  names(.lab_pred)
)
if (length(.unlabelled)) {
  stop(
    "Predictors without labels: ",
    paste(.unlabelled, collapse = ", "),
    call. = FALSE
  )
}
## Fail if any observed outcome has no Spanish label
.unknown_outcomes <- setdiff(
  unique(.plot_source$Outcome_key),
  c("Readmission", "Death")
)
if (length(.unknown_outcomes)) {
  stop(
    "Outcomes without labels: ",
    paste(.unknown_outcomes, collapse = ", "),
    call. = FALSE
  )
}
## Report labels that have no corresponding observations
.absent <- setdiff(
  names(.lab_pred),
  unique(.plot_source$Predictor_key)
)
if (length(.absent)) {
  message(
    "Labels defined without data: ",
    paste(.absent, collapse = ", ")
  )
}
## Assign final labels and preserve the intended panel order
.plot_df <- .plot_source |>
  dplyr::mutate(
    Predictor_lab = factor(
      unname(.lab_pred[Predictor_key]),
      levels = unname(.lab_pred)
    ),
    Desenlace = factor(
      dplyr::recode(
        Outcome_key,
        Readmission = "Readmisión",
        Death = "Mortalidad"
      ),
      levels = c("Readmisión", "Mortalidad")
    )
  )
## Verify that no observations were lost or left without labels
stopifnot(
  "Some rows remain without a predictor label" = !anyNA(.plot_df$Predictor_lab),
  "Some rows remain without an outcome label" = !anyNA(.plot_df$Desenlace),
  "Rows were lost while assigning labels" = nrow(.plot_df) == nrow(shap_curve_norm)
)
## Print the complete identifier-to-label mapping for audit
.label_audit <- .plot_df |>
  dplyr::distinct(
    Predictor_original,
    Predictor_key,
    Predictor_lab
  ) |>
  dplyr::arrange(Predictor_lab)
print(.label_audit, n = Inf)
## 2. Plot ----------------------------------------------------------------------
.cols <- c(
  "Readmisión" = "#1f77b4",
  "Mortalidad" = "#d62728"
)
p_funcform <- ggplot2::ggplot(
  .plot_df,
  ggplot2::aes(
    x = x_norm,
    y = shap_median,
    colour = Desenlace,
    fill = Desenlace
  )
) +
  ggplot2::geom_hline(

    linetype = "dashed",
    colour = "grey55",

  ) +

    ),
    alpha = 0.15,
    colour = NA
  ) +
  ggplot2::geom_line(linewidth = 0.7) +
  ggplot2::geom_point(size = 0.7) +
  ggplot2::facet_wrap(
    ~ Predictor_lab,
    ncol = 4,
    scales = "free_y"
  ) +
  ggplot2::scale_x_continuous(
    labels = scales::percent_format(accuracy = 1),
    limits = c(0, 1),
    breaks = c(0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1)
  ) +
  ggplot2::scale_colour_manual(values = .cols) +
  ggplot2::scale_fill_manual(values = .cols) +
  ggplot2::labs(
    x = "Percentil normalizado del predictor (0 = bajo, 1 = alto)",
    y = "Influencia SHAP (mediana e IQR)",
    colour = NULL,
    fill = NULL
  ) +
  ggplot2::theme_minimal(base_size = 9) +
  ggplot2::theme(
    legend.position = "top",
    strip.text = ggplot2::element_text(
      size = 7.5,
      face = "bold"
    ),
    panel.grid.minor = ggplot2::element_blank(),
    panel.spacing = grid::unit(0.6, "lines")
  )

    "mental health, physical health, transgression), 0% high achievement to 100% minimal achievement. ",

# helper: 3-level ordinal score (0 ref, 1 intermedio, 2 minimo)
make_3level_score <- function(intermedio, minimo) {
  dplyr::case_when(
    minimo == 1 ~ 2,
    intermedio == 1 ~ 1,
    TRUE ~ 0
  )
}
add_boot_linear_terms <- function(df) {
  df %>%
    dplyr::mutate(
      eva_consumo_score     = eva_consumo_logro_intermedio * 1 + eva_consumo_logro_minimo * 2,
      eva_ocupacion_score   = eva_ocupacion_logro_intermedio * 1 + eva_ocupacion_logro_minimo * 2,
      eva_fisica_score      = eva_fisica_logro_intermedio * 1 + eva_fisica_logro_minimo * 2,
      eva_relinterp_score   = eva_relinterp_logro_intermedio * 1 + eva_relinterp_logro_minimo * 2,
      eva_sm_score          = eva_sm_logro_intermedio * 1 + eva_sm_logro_minimo * 2,
      eva_transgnorma_score = eva_transgnorma_logro_intermedio * 1 + eva_transgnorma_logro_minimo * 2,
      eva_fam_score         = eva_fam_logro_intermedio * 1 + eva_fam_logro_minimo * 2,
      ed_attainment_score   = ed_attainment_corr_2_completed_high_school_or_less * 1 +
                              ed_attainment_corr_3_completed_primary_school_or_less * 2,
      prim_sub_freq_score   = prim_sub_freq_rec_2_2_6_days_wk * 1 + prim_sub_freq_rec_3_daily * 2
    )
}
py_corrected_datasets_boot <- lapply(py_corrected_datasets, add_boot_linear_terms)

gc()
# Functional-form candidates for the ds63-SELECTED continuous readmission predictors ONLY:
# age (adm_age_rec3) and treatment duration (dit_m). porc_pobr is NOT in the DeepSurv readmit
# model, so no poverty functional form is tested. Built on the post-NPH base WITHOUT
# interaction crossings (interactions are handled in the Interactions section).
# PRIORITY: clear concave curve (quad_gain 0.18)
form_readmit_ds_age_poly2 <- update(formula_shap_ds_readmit_clean_updated, . ~ . - adm_age_rec3 + poly(adm_age_rec3, 2))
form_readmit_ds_age_ns3   <- update(formula_shap_ds_readmit_clean_updated, . ~ . - adm_age_rec3 + splines::ns(adm_age_rec3, df = 3))
# dit_m casi lineal: solo cola derecha -> probar log/ns, esperar nada (xgb3 ya las empeoró)
form_readmit_ds_dit_log   <- update(formula_shap_ds_readmit_clean_updated, . ~ . - dit_m + log(dit_m + 1))
form_readmit_ds_dit_ns3   <- update(formula_shap_ds_readmit_clean_updated, . ~ . - dit_m + splines::ns(dit_m, df = 3))

[chunk: shap-criteria-func-form-cat-vs-cont] 0.0 sec (0.0 min)
Code 
.t0 <- proc.time()
# Ordinal (0/1/2) vs categorical-dummy test, ONE predictor at a time, for the ds63-SELECTED
# ordinal families in the readmission model: eva_consumo, eva_sm, ed_attainment.
# Decision is on C-index/IBS (eval_times-matched baseline); AICc is for record only.
score_map <- list(
  eva_consumo = list(
    cont = "eva_consumo_score",
    cat  = c("eva_consumo_logro_intermedio", "eva_consumo_logro_minimo")
  ),
  eva_sm = list(
    cont = "eva_sm_score",
    cat  = c("eva_sm_logro_intermedio", "eva_sm_logro_minimo")
  ),
  ed_attainment = list(
    cont = "ed_attainment_score",
    cat  = c(
      "ed_attainment_corr_2_completed_high_school_or_less",
      "ed_attainment_corr_3_completed_primary_school_or_less"
    )
  )
)
base_readmit <- formula_shap_ds_readmit_clean_updated
base_death   <- formula_shap_ds_death_updated

aic_cat <- compute_mi_aicc(base_readmit, py_corrected_datasets_boot)
aic_cat_val <- aic_cat$mean_aicc
cat("Base categorical AICc:", aic_cat_val, "\n\n")

results_list <- list()
for (nm in names(score_map)) {
  m <- score_map[[nm]]
  cat("\n>>> Processing:", nm, "<<<\n")
  f_readmit_cont <- replace_cat_with_continuous(base_readmit, m$cat, m$cont)
  aic_cont <- compute_mi_aicc(f_readmit_cont, py_corrected_datasets_boot)
  aic_cont_val <- aic_cont$mean_aicc
  cat("  AICc categorical:", aic_cat_val, "| continuous:", aic_cont_val,
      "| delta:", round(aic_cont_val - aic_cat_val, 3), "\n")
  gc()
  res <- run_dualcox_score_comparison(
    formula_readmit = f_readmit_cont,
    formula_death   = base_death,
    imputed_list    = py_corrected_datasets_boot,
    k_folds         = 5,
    eval_times      = c(3, 6, 12, 36, 60),
    scores          = c("lp", "risk"),
    within_strata   = FALSE
  )
  results_list[[nm]] <- list(result = res, aic_cat = aic_cat_val,
                             aic_cont = aic_cont_val, aic_delta = aic_cont_val - aic_cat_val)
  cat("  Done.\n")
}
summary_df <- dplyr::bind_rows(lapply(names(results_list), function(nm) {
  entry <- results_list[[nm]]
  entry$result$comparison_summary |>
    dplyr::filter(Time == "Global") |>
    dplyr::mutate(model = nm, aic_cat = entry$aic_cat,
                  aic_cont = entry$aic_cont, aic_delta = entry$aic_delta)
}))
if (exists("results_ds_shap_py_upd")) {
  base_df <- (if ("comparison_summary" %in% names(results_ds_shap_py_upd)) {
    results_ds_shap_py_upd$comparison_summary
  } else {
    results_ds_shap_py_upd$summary |> dplyr::mutate(score_type = "lp")
  }) |>
    dplyr::filter(Time == "Global") |>
    dplyr::mutate(model = "categorical_base", aic_cat = aic_cat_val,
                  aic_cont = NA_real_, aic_delta = NA_real_)
  summary_df <- dplyr::bind_rows(base_df, summary_df)
}
summary_df |>
  dplyr::filter(Risk == "Readmission") |>
  dplyr::select(model, score_type, Metric, mean, sd, q025, q975, n, aic_cat, aic_cont, aic_delta) |>
  dplyr::arrange(Metric, model, score_type) |>
  knitr::kable("markdown", caption = "Dual-Cox: one-at-a-time continuous replacement (readmission, ds63-selected ordinals)") |> 
  print()

    cat  = c("eva_sm_logro_intermedio", "eva_sm_logro_minimo")
  ),
  eva_ocupacion = list(
    cont = "eva_ocupacion_score",
    cat  = c("eva_ocupacion_logro_intermedio", "eva_ocupacion_logro_minimo")
  ),
  eva_fisica = list(
    cont = "eva_fisica_score",
    cat  = c("eva_fisica_logro_intermedio", "eva_fisica_logro_minimo")
  ),
  ed_attainment = list(
    cont = "ed_attainment_score",
    cat  = c(
      "ed_attainment_corr_2_completed_high_school_or_less",
      "ed_attainment_corr_3_completed_primary_school_or_less"
    )
  ),
  prim_sub_freq = list(
    cont = "prim_sub_freq_score",
    cat  = c("prim_sub_freq_rec_2_2_6_days_wk", "prim_sub_freq_rec_3_daily")
  )
)

aic_cat <- compute_mi_aicc(base_death, py_corrected_datasets_boot)
aic_cat_val <- aic_cat$mean_aicc
cat("Base categorical AICc (death):", aic_cat_val, "\n\n")

results_list_death <- list()
for (nm in names(score_map_death)) {
  m <- score_map_death[[nm]]
  cat("\n>>> Processing:", nm, "<<<\n")
  f_death_cont <- replace_cat_with_continuous(formula = base_death, cat_vars = m$cat, cont_var = m$cont)
  aic_cont <- compute_mi_aicc(f_death_cont, py_corrected_datasets_boot)
  aic_cont_val <- aic_cont$mean_aicc
  cat("  AICc categorical:", aic_cat_val, "| continuous:", aic_cont_val,
      "| delta:", round(aic_cont_val - aic_cat_val, 3), "\n")
  gc()
  res <- run_dualcox_score_comparison(
    formula_readmit = base_readmit,
    formula_death   = f_death_cont,
    imputed_list    = py_corrected_datasets_boot,
    k_folds         = 5,
    eval_times      = c(3, 6, 12, 36, 60),
    scores          = c("lp", "risk"),
    within_strata   = FALSE
  )
  results_list_death[[nm]] <- list(result = res, aic_cat = aic_cat_val,
                                   aic_cont = aic_cont_val, aic_delta = aic_cont_val - aic_cat_val)
  cat("  Done.\n")
}
summary_df_death <- dplyr::bind_rows(lapply(names(results_list_death), function(nm) {
  entry <- results_list_death[[nm]]
  entry$result$comparison_summary |>
    dplyr::filter(Time == "Global") |>
    dplyr::mutate(model = nm, aic_cat = entry$aic_cat,
                  aic_cont = entry$aic_cont, aic_delta = entry$aic_delta)
}))
if (exists("results_ds_shap_py_upd")) {
  base_df_death <- results_ds_shap_py_upd$comparison_summary |>
    dplyr::filter(Time == "Global") |>
    dplyr::mutate(model = "categorical_base", aic_cat = aic_cat_val,
                  aic_cont = NA_real_, aic_delta = NA_real_)
  summary_df_death <- dplyr::bind_rows(base_df_death, summary_df_death)
}

~ 50 minutes

No transformation is necessary. The original SHAP-selected formulas with categorical dummies and linear continuous terms are already optimally calibrated for predictive performance. Any functional-form “improvement” trades ~0.02% AICc gain for ~8-25% IBS loss. That is a terrible trade.

We explored alternative functional forms for continuous and ordinal predictors, including polynomial, spline, and monotonic transformations, as well as linear-score representations of ordinal variables. Although some transformations improved model fit according to AICc, they consistently worsened predictive performance as measured by the integrated Brier score. Therefore, we retained the original parameterization, favoring predictive accuracy over in-sample fit.Decision rule: a functional-form variant replaces the categorical/linear baseline only with a validated +0.01 Uno’s C or +0.01 IBS gain (eval_times-matched baseline results_ds_shap_py_upd) without worsening the other metric or calibration. AICc is reported for transparency only. Here no variant clears the bar, so the simpler specification is retained - complexity must earn its place.

.t0 <- proc.time()

# ---- output dir & stem ----
if (grepl("Escritorio", project_root)) {
  out_dir  <-file.path(gsub("Escritorio2025", "Escritorio", project_root), "data", "20241015_out")
} else {
  out_dir  <-file.path(project_root, "data", "20241015_out")
}
date_sfx <- format(Sys.Date(), "%Y_%m_%d")
stem     <- paste0("pred24_ndp_", date_sfx)
dir.create(out_dir, recursive = TRUE, showWarnings = FALSE)
# ---- helpers: individual RDS instead of one big .RData ----
.strip_raw <- function(obj) {
  if (!is.list(obj)) return(obj)
  lapply(obj, function(m) { m$raw_predictions <- NULL; m })
}
.safe_save <- function(obj_name, label = obj_name, strip = FALSE) {
  if (!exists(obj_name, envir = .GlobalEnv, inherits = FALSE)) {
    cat(sprintf("  SKIP  %-42s (not found)\n", label)); return(invisible(NULL))
  }
  obj  <- get(obj_name, envir = .GlobalEnv)
  if (strip) obj <- .strip_raw(obj)
  path <- file.path(out_dir, paste0(stem, "__", label, ".rds"))
  tryCatch({
    saveRDS(obj, path)
    cat(sprintf("  OK    %-42s -> %s\n", label, basename(path)))
  }, error = function(e) {
    cat(sprintf("  ERROR %-42s : %s\n", label, conditionMessage(e)))
  })
}
cat(paste0("\n=== Saving pred24 (DeepSurv sensitivity) results -- ", date_sfx, " ===\n\n"))
# ---- Selected formulas (fixed rule, DeepSurv) ----
.safe_save("sel_rule_ds",                  "sel_rule_ds")
.safe_save("formula_shap_ds_readmit_rule", "formula_readmit_ds_rule")
.safe_save("formula_shap_ds_death_rule",   "formula_death_ds_rule")
# ---- Dual Cox python-style (Uno's C + IBS); strip raw predictions ----
.safe_save("results_ds_shap_py",         "dual_shap_ds_py",      strip = F)   # rule baseline
.safe_save("results_ds_shap_py_upd",     "dual_shap_ds_upd_py",  strip = F)   # +strata
.safe_save("results_ds_shap_py_upd_int", "dual_shap_ds_int_py",  strip = F)   # +interactions
# ---- AICc (for record only) ----
.safe_save("aicc_readmit_shap_ds",     "aicc_readmit_shap_ds")
.safe_save("aicc_death_shap_ds",       "aicc_death_shap_ds")
.safe_save("aicc_readmit_upd1_shap",   "aicc_readmit_shap_ds_strata")
.safe_save("aicc_death_upd1_shap",     "aicc_death_shap_ds_strata")
.safe_save("aicc_readmit_shap_int_ds", "aicc_readmit_shap_ds_int")
.safe_save("aicc_death_shap_int_ds",   "aicc_death_shap_ds_int")
# ---- Calibration results (AJ for readmission, 1-KM for death) ----
.safe_save("cal_readmit_shap_ds",      "cal_readmit_shap_ds")
.safe_save("cal_death_shap_ds",        "cal_death_shap_ds")
.safe_save("cal_readmit_shap2_ds",     "cal_readmit_shap_ds2")
.safe_save("cal_death_shap2_ds",       "cal_death_shap_ds2")
